# Total Bugs — Detection-Logic Bypass Findings Catalog

A machine-extracted catalog of **1116 distinct detection-rule bypass findings** mined from analysis of public detection rulesets (Elastic Security, Microsoft Sentinel, Sigma), mapped to the ADE taxonomy. These are the findings behind the worked technique files in the five subcategories below.

> Findings are LLM-generated analysis of public vendor rules and are provided for defensive detection engineering. Each row names the affected rule, the ADE bug, the bypass, and a remediation. Deduplicated by (rule, subcategory, bypass).

**By platform:** Elastic 514 · Sentinel 575 · Sigma 27 · **Total 1116**

**By subcategory:** [ADE2-02](#ade2-02-versioning) 210 · [ADE2-03](#ade2-03-locations) 153 · [ADE2-04](#ade2-04-file-types) 67 · [ADE3-02](#ade3-02-aggregation-hijacking) 407 · [ADE3-03](#ade3-03-timing-and-scheduling) 279

---
